Defaults\n    32→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n    33→BASE = Path(\"/Volumes/synologyNFS/server-manager/projects/mardov\")\n    34→DEFAULT_JOBS = BASE / \"production\" / \"panel-jobs.json\"\n    35→DEFAULT_PANELS = BASE / \"assets/pilots/pilot-a/panels\"\n    36→DEFAULT_OUTPUT = BASE / \"assets/pilots/pilot-a\"\n    37→\n    38→TARGET_WIDTH = 1200\n 18, 22)\n    45→TITLE_BG = (30, 30, 38)\n    46→TITLE_FG = (220, 200, 160)\n    47→TEXT_FG = (200, 200, 200)\n    48→MISSING_BG = (50, 50, 55)\n    49→MISSING_FG = (140, 140, 140)\n    50→\n    51→\n    52→def parse_args():\n    53→    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\"Assemble panel images into a showable draft\")\n    54→    p.add_argument(\"--jobs\", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_JOBS,\n    55→                   help=\"Path to panel-jobs.json\")\n    56→    p.add_argument(\"--panels\", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_PANELS,\n    57→                   help=\"Directory containing panel images\")\n    58→    p.add_argument(\"--output\", type=Path, default=DEFAULT_OUTPUT,\n    59→                   help=\"Output directory\")\n    60→    p.add_argument(\"--width\", type=int, default=TARGET_WIDTH,\n    61→                   help=\"Target width in pixels (default: 1200)\")\n    62→    p.add_argument(\"--no-image\", action=\"store_true\",\n    63→                   help=\"Skip PNG generation (HTML only)\")\n    64→    p.add_argument(\"--no-html\", action=\"store_true\",\n    65→                   help=\"Skip HTML generation (PNG only)\")\n    66→    p.add_argument(\"--narration\", action=\"store_true\",\n    67→                   help=\"Include narrative text below panels\")\n    68→    return p.parse_args()\n    69→\n    70→\n    71→def load_jobs(jobs_path: Path) -> list:\n    72→    with open(jobs_path) as f:\n    73→        return json.load(f)\n    74→\n    75→\n    76→def extract_title(filename: str) -> str:\n    77→    \"\"\"Extract human-readable title from filename.\n    78→\n    79→    panel-01-conquis-abbey.png -> '01 - Conquis Abbey'\n    80→    panel-31-a-vignette-quick-cuts-three-figures.png -> '31a - Vignette Quick Cuts ...'\n    81→    \"\"\"\n    82→    stem = Path(filename).stem\n    83→    stem = re.sub(r'^panel-', '', stem)\n    84→    stem = re.sub(r'-draft$', '', stem)\n    85→    m = re.match(r'^(\\d+)(?:-([a-d]))?-(.+)$', stem)\n    86→    if m:\n    87→        num, suffix, name = m.group(1), m.group(2) or '', m.group(3)\n    88→        name = name.replace('-', ' ').title()\n    89→        return f\"{num}{suffix} - {name}\"\n    90→    return stem.replace('-', ' ').title()\n    91→\n    92→\n    93→def extract_narration(prompt_original: str) -> str:\n    94→    \"\"\"Strip style/tone suffixes from the original prompt to get narrative text.\"\"\"\n    95→    if not prompt_original:\n    96→        return \"\"\n    97→    raw = prompt_original\n    98→    for marker in [\"Gothic anime style\", \"gothic anime style\"]:\n    99→        idx = raw.find(marker)\n   100→        if idx > 0:\n   101→            raw = raw[:idx].rstrip(\". ,\")\n   102→            break\n   103→    return raw\n   104→\n   105→\n   106→def find_image(job: dict, panels_dir: Path) -> \"Path | None\":\n   107→    \"\"\"Find the best image file for a panel job.\n   108→\n   109→    Priority: exact non-draft > polished > any non-draft > draft of canonical > any draft.\n   110→    Skips BEFORE/AFTER comparison images.\n   111→    \"\"\"\n   112→    canonical = job[\"filename\"]\n   113→    panel_num = job[\"panel\"]\n   114→    stem = Path(canonical).stem  # e.g. panel-01-conquis-abbey\n   115→\n   116→    # 1. Exact canonical file (non-draft, highest quality)\n   117→    exact = panels_dir / canonical\n   118→    if exact.exists():\n   119→        return exact\n   120→\n   121→    num_str = f\"{panel_num:02d}\" if isinstance(panel_num, int) else str(panel_num)\n   122→\n   123→    # Detect if this job has a letter suffix (e.g. panel-31-a-...)\n   124→    suffix_match = re.match(r'^panel-\\d+-([a-d])-.+\\.png$', canonical)\n   125→    letter = suffix_match.group(1) if suffix_match else None\n   126→\n   127→    # Collect all panel files matching this number\n   128→    if letter:\n   129→        # Match both \"panel-31-a-...\" and \"panel-31a-...\" naming conventions\n   130→        pattern = re.compile(\n   131→            rf'^panel-{re.escape(num_str)}-?{re.escape(letter)}-.+\\.png$',\n   132→            re.IGNORECASE,\n   133→        )\n   134→    else:\n   135→        # Match panel number but not letter-suffixed variants\n   136→        pattern = re.compile(\n   137→            rf'^panel-{re.escape(num_str)}-(?![a-d]-)(.+)\\.png$',\n   138→            re.IGNORECASE,\n   139→        )\n   140→\n   141→    candidates = [f for f in panels_dir.iterdir() if pattern.match(f.name)]\n   142→\n   143→    # Filter out BEFORE/AFTER comparison images\n   144→    candidates = [\n   145→        f for f in candidates\n   146→        if '-BEFORE' not in f.name and '-AFTER' not in f.name\n   147→    ]\n   148→\n   149→    non_draft = [f for f in candidates if '-draft' not in f.stem]\n   150→    draft = [f for f in candidates if '-draft' in f.stem]\n   151→\n   152→    # Prefer polished > any non-draft\n   153→    polished = [f for f in non_draft if 'polished' in f.stem.lower()]\n   154→    if polished:\n   155→        return polished[0]\n   156→    if non_draft:\n   157→        return non_draft[0]\n   158→\n   159→    # Draft version of the canonical name\n   160→    draft_canonical = panels_dir / (stem + \"-draft.png\")\n   161→    if draft_canonical.exists():\n   162→        return draft_canonical\n   163→\n   164→    # Any draft for this panel\n   165→    if draft:\n   166→        return draft[0]\n   167→\n   168→    return None\n   169→\n   170→\n   171→def get_font(size: int):\n   172→    \"\"\"Load a system font, falling back to Pillow default.\"\"\"\n   173→    for fp in [\n   174→        \"/System/Library/Fonts/Helvetica.ttc\",\n   175→        \"/System/Library/Fonts/SFNSMono.ttf\",\n   176→        \"/System/Library/Fonts/Menlo.ttc\",\n   177→        \"/usr/share/fonts/truetype/dejavu/DejaVuSans.ttf\",\n   178→    ]:\n   179→        if os.path.exists(fp):\n   180→            try:\n   181→                return ImageFont.truetype(fp, size)\n   182→            except Exception:\n   183→                continue\n   184→    return ImageFont.load_default()\n   185→\n   186→\n   187→def wrap_text(text: str, font, max_width: int, draw: ImageDraw.ImageDraw) -> list:\n   188→    \"\"\"Word-wrap text to fit within max_width pixels.\"\"\"\n   189→    words = text.split()\n   190→    lines = []\n   191→    current = \"\"\n   192→    for word in words:\n   193→        test = f\"{current} {word}\".strip()\n   194→        bbox = draw.textbbox((0, 0), test, font=font)\n   195→        if bbox[2] - bbox[0] <= max_width:\n   196→            current = test\n   197→        else:\n   198→            if current:\n   199→                lines.append(current)\n   200→            current = word\n   201→    if current:\n   202→        lines.append(current)\n   203→    return lines or [\"\"]\n   204→\n   205→\n   206→def render_panel_block(\n   207→    job: dict,\n   208→    panels_dir: Path,\n   209→    target_width: int,\n   210→ The architecture is beautiful and crumbling simultaneously: flying buttresses still hold but show cracks, stained glass windows glow faintly from candlelight within, stone gargoyles eroded by centuries of salt wind. Ravens circle the bell tower in a slow gyre. Mist rolls in from the ocean, obscuring the base of the cliff. The sky is heavy, overcast, pressing down. Gothic anime style, motion webtoon panel, establishing shot. Emotional tone: ancient weight — this place has seen more than it should, and it is tired.\"\n     9→  },\n    10→  {\n    11→    \"panel\": 2,\n    12→    \"filename\": \"panel-02-abbey-interior-war-room.png\",\n    13→    \"prompt\": \"masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, interior, gothic stone hall, vaulted ceiling, long oak table covered in maps and correspondence, candelabras with melting candles, military banners on walls, scarlet and gold heraldry, dusty light through stained glass, war planning room, parchment scrolls, wax seals, gothic, dark fantasy, detailed background, warm amber lighting\",\n    14→    \"lora_files\": [],\n    15→    \"lora_info\": \"none\",\n    16→    \"prompt_original\": \"Interior of Conquis Abbey's great hall repurposed as a war room. A long oak table dominates the center, covered in maps, military correspondence, and half-eaten meals — this room has not been cleaned because the people in it have not stopped working. Candelabras with melting candles cast warm uneven light. Military banners in scarlet and gold hang from the vaulted stone ceiling. Dusty colored light filters through cracked stained glass windows depicting saints whose names have been forgotten. The room smells of old stone, tallow, and desperation. Gothic anime style, motion webtoon panel. Emotional tone: the ordinary machinery of crisis — not dramatic, just exhausting.\"\n    17→  },\n    18→  {\n    19→    \"panel\": 3,\n    20→    \"filename\": \"panel-03-abbey-the-summons.png\",\n    21→    \"prompt\": \"masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, interior gothic hall, exhausted messenger kneeling, torn muddy cloak, holding sealed scroll with wax seal, candlelight, officers gathered around war table, tension, urgency, rain-soaked, gothic, dark fantasy, dramatic lighting\",\n    22→    \"lora_files\": [],\n    23→    \"lora_info\": \"none\",\n    24→    \"prompt_original\": \"A messenger kneels at the threshold of the war room, rain-soaked and exhausted, mud on his boots and cloak torn from hard riding. He holds out a sealed scroll — the wax seal is prominent, official, bearing a crest the officers at the table recognize. The officers have frozen mid-conversation, maps forgotten. One hand reaches for the scroll. The candlelight catches the wax seal's impression. Gothic anime style, motion webtoon panel. Emotional tone: the moment before bad news — everyone already knows.\"\n    25→  },\n    26→  {\n    27→    \"panel\": 4,\n    28→    \"filename\": \"panel-04-the-seal.png\",\n    29→    \"prompt\": \"masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, extreme close-up, wax seal on aged parchment, official crest stamped in red wax, candlelight reflecting off wax surface, aged fingers holding parchment edges, gothic, dark fantasy, detailed texture, warm amber lighting\",\n    30→    \"lora_files\": [],\n    31→    \"lora_info\": \"none\",\n    32→    \"prompt_original\": \"Extreme close-up of the wax seal on the scroll. Red wax bearing an official military crest — the kind of seal that means mobilization, not correspondence. The parchment around it is travel-worn, water-stained at the edges. Aged fingers hold the scroll's edges, about to break the seal. Candlelight reflects off the wax surface, making the crest seem alive. Gothic anime style, motion webtoon panel. Emotional tone: bureaucratic dread — the seal is beautiful and it means people will die.\"\n    33→  },\n    34→  {\n    35→    \"panel\": 5,\n    36→    \"filename\": \"panel-05-the-order.png\",\n    37→    \"prompt\": \"masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, interior gothic hall, officer reading scroll aloud, grim expression, other officers listening with hardened faces, candlelight, maps on table, military atmosphere, parchment unfurled, gothic, dark fantasy, dramatic lighting\",\n    38→    \"lora_files\": [],\n    39→    \"lora_info\": \"none\",\n    40→    \"prompt_original\": \"An officer reads the scroll aloud to the assembled war room. His expression is grim, controlled — he has delivered bad news before. The others listen with faces that have heard too much bad news to react. One officer's hand rests on a map, fingers curled. Another stares at the candle flame. The scroll's text is not readable but its length implies complexity — this is not a simple order. Gothic anime style, motion webtoon panel. Emotional tone: professionals absorbing impact without showing it.\"\n    41→  },\n    42→  {\n    43→    \"panel\": 6,\n    44→    \"filename\": \"panel-06-the-map.png\",\n    45→    \"prompt\": \"masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, top-down view, aged fantasy map on oak table, candlelight, red markers showing threat positions, ink annotations, compass rose, coastal region with towns marked, finger pointing at location, parchment texture, gothic, dark fantasy, warm lighting\",\n    46→    \"lora_files\": [],\n    47→    \"lora_info\": \"none\",\n    48→    \"prompt_original\": \"Top-down view of the war table map. Candlelight makes the map's edges glow amber. Gothic anime style, motion webtoon panel. Emotional tone: the geometry of danger made visible — the town is surrounded and does not know it yet.\"\n    49→  },\n    50→  {\n    51→    \"panel\": 7,\n    52→    \"filename\": \"panel-07-abbey-night-decision.png\",\n    53→    \"prompt\": \"masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, exterior, gothic abbey at night, single lit window glowing amber against dark stone, moonlight on ocean waves below cliff, ravens roosting on gargoyles, stars partially visible through clouds, solitary figure silhouetted in window, gothic, dark fantasy, atmospheric, night scene\",\n    54→    \"lora_files\": [],\n    55→    \"lora_info\": \"none\",\n    56→    \"prompt_original\": \"Exterior of Conquis Abbey at night. Gothic anime style, motion webtoon panel. Emotional tone: the loneliness of command — one light against all that dark.\"\n    57→  },\n    58→  {\n    59→    \"panel\": 8,\n    60→    \"filename\": \"panel-08-dawn-riders-depart.png\",\n    61→    \"prompt\": \"masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, wide shot, dawn, riders on horseback departing abbey gates, silhouetted against sunrise, dusty road winding down from cliff, scarlet cloaks, military bearing, horses breath visible in cold air, abbey behind them growing smaller, gothic, dark fantasy, dramatic lighting, golden hour\",\n    62→    \"lora_files\": [],\n    63→    \"lora_info\": \"none\",\n    64→    \"prompt_original\": \"Dawn. Emotional tone: departure as sacrifice — they ride toward something they may not survive, and the sunrise is beautiful anyway.\"\n    65→  },\n    66→  {\n    67→    \"panel\": 9,\n    68→    \"filename\": \"panel-09-the-road.png\",\n    69→    \"prompt\": \"masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, wide landscape, winding road through dark forest, riders on horseback small in frame, autumn trees with falling leaves, overcast sky, stone bridge over stream, crows perched on dead tree, atmosphere of journey, gothic, dark fantasy, detailed background\",\n    70→    \"lora_files\": [],\n    71→    \"lora_info\": \"none\",\n    72→    \"prompt_original\": \"Wide landscape panel showing the road. Emotional tone: the world does not care about your mission — it is vast and you are small.\"\n    73→  },\n    74→  {\n    75→    \"panel\": 10,\n    76→    \"filename\": \"panel-10-roadside-discovery.png\",\n    77→    \"prompt\": \"masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, abandoned merchant cart on road, overturned, scattered goods, claw marks on wood, no bodies visible, riders stopped and examining, dark forest backdrop, tension, investigation, gothic, dark fantasy, ominous atmosphere\",\n    78→    \"lora_files\": [],\n    79→    \"lora_info\": \"none\",\n    80→    \"prompt_original\": \"The riders have stopped. One rider has dismounted and kneels to examine the marks. The dark forest presses in from both sides. Something happened here recently. Gothic anime style, motion webtoon panel. Emotional tone: the first evidence that the threat is not distant — it is here, and it was hungry.\"\n    81→  },\n    82→  {\n    83→    \"panel\": 11,\n    84→    \"filename\": \"panel-11-port-town-first-sight.png\",\n    85→    \"prompt\": \"masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, wide establishing shot, medieval port town from hillside approach, stone buildings clustered around harbor, wooden docks, ships at anchor, overcast sky, smoke from chimneys, town walls with visible siege damage and repair, riders approaching on road above, gothic, dark fantasy, detailed background\",\n    86→    \"lora_files\": [],\n    87→    \"lora_info\": \"none\",\n    88→    \"prompt_original\": \"Wide establishing shot from a hillside. Full output saved to: /Users/eviemini/.claude/projects/-Users-eviemini-Documents-code-server-manager/a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08/tool-results/toolu_01EuMLfiiceQfQGsEeu4gUNq.txt\n\nPreview (first 2KB):\n     1→# Mardov Pilot A -- Story Bible\n     2→\n     3→**Series**: Heroes of Mardov\n     4→**Format**: Gothic anime motion webtoon (vertical scroll)\n     5→**Pilot Length**: 80 panels across 7 acts\n     6→**Visual Style**: Dark fantasy, manhwa ink lines, Illustrious XL 2.0 + gothic LoRA stack\n     7→**Aspect Ratio**: 832x1216 (2:3 webtoon tall)\n     8→\n     9→---\n    10→\n    11→## Logline\n    12→\n    13→When a band of strangers answers a military summons to a besieged port town, they are led into an ancient dungeon beneath the streets -- and into a betrayal that reveals a sealed door bearing the crest of the Raven Queen, a god who should not have business with the living.\n    14→\n    15→---\n    16→\n    17→## Synopsis\n    18→\n    19→On a windswept coastal cliff, Conquis Abbey receives an urgent military summons. Riders depart at dawn, traveling through a darkening landscape scarred by something predatory, and arrive at a port town bracing for a threat it cannot name. Within the town, a secret forge produces a weapon that burns with unnatural light -- a god-killer blade whose very existence hints at divine stakes the townspeople cannot comprehend.\n    20→\n    21→As dusk settles, we meet the adventurers who will form our party: Sprocket, a gnome diviner reading fate in his cramped carriage study; Brigit, a druid who shifts from wolf to woman at the town walls; Stalwart, a golden-scaled dragonborn paladin who commands attention on every street; and Nyx, a tiefling warlock scribbling secrets in a candlelit inn room. Three more figures flash past in quick cuts -- a wild-eyed mage losing control of her own magic, a furiously short man elbowing through crowds, and a gnome with clever hands and too many pockets. All roads lead to the same recruitment yard.\n    22→\n...\n</persisted-output>"}]},"uuid":"c0f6bb9b-18eb-4ac5-bd72-ef64b2378ea4","timestamp":"2026-03-18T03:08:46.247Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"7856664d-8fff-4d42-803e-1cb70d39a02c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"acff45d0-916e-43ee-a31d-83d38805d1f4","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"00d91d86-ad69-404a-aad8-2a048e6b309c","agentId":"a47eb08dc424f0771","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01KdpRKPpfxTNrMhCHP58AMS","type":"tool_result","content":"# Mardov Pilot A -- Prompt Refinements\n\n**Generated**: 2026-03-17\n**Method**: Cross-reference of panel-jobs.json against canon-reconciliation.md, character-visual-guide.md, pilot-story-bible.md, visual-moments-catalog.md, and combat-choreography.md\n\n**Panel count**: 85 panels (1-80, with 31a-c and 79a-d)\n**Panels needing refinement**: 24\n**Priority breakdown**: HIGH: 7 | MEDIUM: 11 | LOW: 6\n\n---\n\n## Panel 09 -- The Road\n**Current prompt summary**: `wide landscape, winding road through dark forest, riders on horseback small in frame, autumn trees with falling leaves, ove...`\n**Issues**:\n- Canon lock specifies: \"necromancers moved into the Old Forest -- the trees should show signs of blight.\" Current prompt says \"autumn trees with falling leaves\" and \"crows perched on dead tree\" -- this reads as natural autumn, not unnatural blight.\n- The distinction matters: this is the first environmental hint that something supernatural is wrong with the land. Autumn leaves are seasonal; blight is sinister.\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, wide landscape, winding road through dark forest, riders on horseback small in frame, trees showing unnatural blight, blackened bark, leaves withering unnaturally, grey-green sickly foliage, overcast sky, stone bridge over stream, crows perched on dead tree, atmosphere of journey through a wounded landscape, gothic, dark fantasy, detailed background`\n**Priority**: MEDIUM\n\n---\n\n## Panel 10 -- Roadside Discovery\n**Current prompt summary**: `abandoned merchant cart on road, overturned, scattered goods, claw marks on wood, no bodies visible, riders stopped and...`\n**Issues**:\n- Canon lock says: \"kobolds can fly. The claw marks should be overhead -- on tree branches, not just on the cart. Something came from above.\" The current prompt only mentions claw marks on the cart wood. Missing the overhead/aerial attack evidence entirely.\n- This is a foreshadowing beat for the flying kobolds in Panel 44. The claw marks on tree branches are a visual plant.\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, abandoned merchant cart on road, overturned, scattered goods, claw marks gouged into wood, deep claw marks on overhead tree branches, attack came from above, no bodies visible, riders stopped and examining, one looking up at branch damage, dark forest backdrop, tension, investigation, gothic, dark fantasy, ominous atmosphere`\n**Priority**: HIGH\n\n---\n\n## Panel 12 -- Town Gate\n**Current prompt summary**: `medieval town gate, stone archway with iron portcullis raised, guards checking travelers, queue of merchants and refugees...`\n**Issues**:\n- Canon lock says: \"Per Summary 9: Argos had nightly attacks on North and East gates. The guards should look exhausted.\" Current prompt says \"guards in mismatched armor checking travelers\" but does not convey exhaustion or the siege-stress.\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, medieval town gate, stone archway with iron portcullis raised, exhausted guards in mismatched armor checking travelers, dark circles under eyes, queue of merchants and refugees, riders approaching, scarlet banners flanking gate, worn cobblestones, busy street beyond, repaired siege damage on walls, gothic, dark fantasy, detailed background`\n**Priority**: LOW\n\n---\n\n## Panel 13 -- Town Preparations\n**Current prompt summary**: `medieval town street, citizens preparing for siege, boarding up windows, carrying supplies, soldiers drilling in backgro...`\n**Issues**:\n- Canon lock says: \"Per Summary 10: the town guard is one man (Mandu). The soldiers are Brigade members, not regulars.\" Current prompt says \"soldiers drilling in background\" which reads as a proper military garrison. The canon is that these are private military contractors (Scarlet Tunic Brigade), not town soldiers.\n- Also: \"civilians pretending to be soldiers\" is canon -- the drill should look ragged, not professional.\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, medieval town street, citizens preparing for siege, boarding up windows, carrying supplies, scarlet-uniformed brigade members drilling civilians in background, ragged uncoordinated drill formation, children watching from doorways, tense atmosphere, overcast, stone buildings, gothic, dark fantasy, daily life under threat`\n**Priority**: LOW\n\n---\n\n## Panel 14 -- Notice Board\n**Current prompt summary**: `close-up, wooden notice board on stone wall, multiple overlapping parchment notices, one prominent recruitment poster w...`\n**Issues**:\n- Canon lock says: \"Per Summary 10: 'about 100 children have been kidnapped in the last two years.' Missing person notices should be for children. This is a deep-lore plant.\" Current prompt mentions \"missing persons\" generically but does not specify children. This is a critical foreshadowing detail -- the missing children connect to the soul-harvesting plot.\n- The recruitment poster and missing-children notices side by side is the visual thesis of Panel 14: the infrastructure of desperation.\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, close-up, wooden notice board on stone wall, multiple overlapping parchment notices, several missing child notices with small sketched portraits, one prominent recruitment poster with official seal, nails and torn remnants of old notices, ink fading on older posts, hand reaching to read recruitment poster, medieval town square background, gothic, dark fantasy, warm overcast lighting`\n**Priority**: HIGH\n\n---\n\n## Panel 21 -- Sprocket Vignette: The Study\n**Current prompt summary**: `1boy, gnome, short stature, tan skin, golden spiked hair, large dark eyes, gap teeth, bushy sideburns, brass goggles o...`\n**Issues**:\n- Character visual guide says: \"wild grey-white hair, yellow pointed hat, large round goggles on forehead, wide grin, big nose, red vest jacket, green and teal accents, white pants, mismatched green buckled boots, brown leather tool belt with pouches, blue crystal-topped staff, golden crystalline geometric wings, brown leather gloves.\"\n- Current prompt says: \"golden spiked hair\" (guide says grey-white), \"burgundy mechanical armor\" (guide says red vest jacket with green/teal accents), missing the yellow pointed hat entirely.\n- The LoRA will push generation toward the reference images, but the prompt text is actively fighting the LoRA by specifying wrong hair color and wrong outfit.\n- Note: The story bible describes Sprocket with \"golden-yellow spiked hair\" which contradicts the visual guide's \"grey-white hair.\" The LoRA reference image is the authority -- it shows grey-white hair. The golden-yellow description may be from an older character version.\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, 1boy, gnome, short stature, tan skin, wild grey-white hair, yellow pointed hat, large round goggles on forehead, large dark eyes, gap teeth, big nose, red vest jacket, green and teal accents, brown leather tool belt with pouches, brown leather gloves, sitting cross-legged, on stack of books, holding glowing crystal, crystal refraction, ghostly face in crystal, interior, cramped carriage study, books stacked, astronomical instruments, arcane diagrams on walls, writing desk, candlelight, warm amber lighting, deep shadows, gothic, dark fantasy, detailed background, dramatic lighting`\n**Priority**: HIGH\n\n---\n\n## Panel 22 -- Sprocket Vignette: The Arrival\n**Current prompt summary**: `1boy, gnome, short stature, tan skin, golden spiked hair, large dark eyes, gap teeth, brass goggles on forehead, burgund...`\n**Issues**:\n- Same character mismatch as Panel 21. Wrong hair color (\"golden spiked\" vs guide's \"grey-white\"), wrong outfit (\"burgundy mechanical armor\" vs \"red vest jacket, green and teal accents\"), missing yellow pointed hat.\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, 1boy, gnome, short stature, tan skin, wild grey-white hair, yellow pointed hat, large round goggles on forehead, large dark eyes, gap teeth, big nose, red vest jacket, green and teal accents, brown leather tool belt with pouches, wide shot, stepping down from carriage, cobblestone street, port town entrance, stone walls, scarlet military banners, wooden docks, grey water, overcast sky, diffuse light, scale contrast, gothic, dark fantasy, detailed background`\n**Priority**: HIGH\n\n---\n\n## Panel 24 -- Brigit Vignette: The Shift\n**Current prompt summary**: `1girl, human, druid, shapeshifting transformation, wolf to human, mid-transformation, traces of grey fur dissolving int...`\n**Issues**:\n- Character visual guide says: \"vibrant red hair partially shaved on one side, green eyes, fair skin with freckles, green bodice with brown leather lacing, dark cloak, leather bracers, holds glowing molten fire orb above palm.\"\n- Current prompt says \"red hair, auburn hair, pale blonde moonlight streaks\" -- the guide has no moonlight streaks and specifies \"partially shaved on one side\" which is a distinctive visual feature missing from the prompt.\n- Outfit mismatch: prompt says \"forest-green leather armor, vine-woven elements\" vs guide's \"green bodice with brown leather lacing, dark cloak, leather bracers.\"\n- Canon lock says the transformation should \"feel like putting on a costume\" and \"The human shape is the disguise.\" The current prompt captures this well emotionally but the visual details fight the LoRA.\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, 1girl, human, druid, shapeshifting transformation, wolf to human, mid-transformation, traces of grey fur dissolving into fair skin with freckles, claws softening into fingers, vibrant red hair partially shaved on one side, green eyes with lingering amber wolf-light, bare feet on cobblestones, green bodice with brown leather lacing, dark cloak, leather bracers, stone town wall shadow, faint amber organic glow, transformation energy, cool shadow vs warm town light, gothic, dark fantasy, fluid transformation`\n**Priority**: HIGH\n\n---\n\n## Panel 25 -- Brigit Vignette: The Town\n**Current prompt summary**: `1girl, young woman, dark auburn hair, pale porcelain skin, green eyes, tattered green druid cloak, bare feet on cobblest...`\n**Issues**:\n- Uses old LoRA file `brigit.safetensors` instead of the newer `brigit-new.safetensors` listed in the visual guide.\n- Missing the \"partially shaved on one side\" hair detail from the visual guide. \"Dark auburn hair\" should be \"vibrant red hair.\"\n- Outfit: \"tattered green druid cloak\" vs guide's \"green bodice with brown leather lacing, dark cloak.\"\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, 1girl, young woman, vibrant red hair partially shaved on one side, fair skin with freckles, green eyes, green bodice with brown leather lacing, dark cloak, leather bracers, bare feet on cobblestones, walking through medieval town street, crowds parting around her, feral awareness, out of place in civilization, market stalls, townsfolk staring, overcast, gothic, dark fantasy, detailed background`\n**Priority**: HIGH (LoRA file also needs updating from brigit.safetensors to brigit-new.safetensors)\n\n---\n\n## Panel 26 -- Brigit Vignette: The Notice\n**Current prompt summary**: `1girl, young woman, dark auburn hair, pale porcelain skin, green eyes, tattered green druid cloak, standing before woode...`\n**Issues**:\n- Same character description mismatches as Panel 25 (wrong hair color/style, wrong outfit, old LoRA).\n- Canon lock says: \"She has 'a soft spot for outcasted children' and always protects the vulnerable despite being punished for it. The missing-children notices from Panel 14 are what hook her.\" The prompt should reference the missing-children posters specifically rather than generic recruitment poster.\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, 1girl, young woman, vibrant red hair partially shaved on one side, fair skin with freckles, green eyes, green bodice with brown leather lacing, dark cloak, leather bracers, standing before wooden notice board, one hand touching missing child notice, recruitment poster beside it, medieval town square, overcast light, pained recognition expression, gothic, dark fantasy, detailed background`\n**Priority**: HIGH (LoRA file also needs updating)\n\n---\n\n## Panel 29 -- Nyx Vignette: The Inn Room\n**Current prompt summary**: `1girl, tiefling, petite, pale pinkish-lavender skin, small curved horns, white voluminous curly hair, large expressive e...`\n**Issues**:\n- Character visual guide says: \"voluminous curly dark purple-violet hair\" for portrait form, not \"white voluminous curly hair.\" The prompt uses white/pale blonde which matches the story bible but contradicts the LoRA reference image (dark purple-violet).\n- Guide also specifies: \"white button-up blouse, dark purple ribbon tie, purple blazer jacket\" for portrait form. Prompt says \"cobalt blue hooded cloak, dark corset.\"\n- The Knight form has \"curly pink-light hair, small horns, pink tail, blue witch hat, blue cloak\" which is closer to the prompt's cobalt blue cloak, but still not a match.\n- Since the LoRA was trained on the portrait form, the prompt should align with it.\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, 1girl, tiefling, petite, pale pinkish-lavender skin, small curved horns, voluminous curly dark purple-violet hair, large expressive dark melancholic eyes, irritated expression, white button-up blouse, dark purple ribbon tie, purple blazer jacket, sitting cross-legged on bed, writing in leather journal, interior, modest inn room, candlelight, golden glow, quill and ink on nightstand, half-eaten apple, barn owl on windowsill, brown and white feathers, grey morning light through curtains, wooden walls, intimate atmosphere, gothic, dark fantasy`\n**Priority**: MEDIUM\n\n---\n\n## Panel 30 -- Nyx Vignette: The Departure\n**Current prompt summary**: `1girl, tiefling, petite, pale pinkish-lavender skin, small curved horns, white voluminous curly hair, pulling cobalt blu...`\n**Issues**:\n- Same hair color mismatch as Panel 29. Should be dark purple-violet per visual guide.\n- Outfit should match the portrait form or Knight form, not a mix of both.\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, 1girl, tiefling, petite, pale pinkish-lavender skin, small curved horns, voluminous curly dark purple-violet hair, pulling hood up, squinting against bright light, purple blazer jacket over white blouse, barn owl landing on shoulder, stepping through inn doorway, warm dark interior behind, bright overcast morning ahead, threshold lighting, reluctant expression, gothic, dark fantasy`\n**Priority**: MEDIUM\n\n---\n\n## Panel 31b -- Quick Cuts: Tiny Rick\n**Current prompt summary**: `1boy, adult human man, very short stature, adult proportions, angular features, elbowing through crowd, daggers at belt...`\n**Issues**:\n- Canon lock adds critical subtext: \"Per DM: '4 feet tall, adult human proportions.' Per player Summary 0-1: he is 'Mordred's next vessel' -- his arc is tragic. The pilot does not know this yet. Daggers at belt. Cornered predator eyes.\"\n- The prompt is visually accurate but misses the canon direction to show \"cornered predator eyes\" -- instead it says \"compressed fury expression.\" These are different emotions: fury is aggressive, cornered predator is defensive-aggressive with an undercurrent of desperation.\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, 1boy, adult human man, very short stature, adult proportions, angular features, elbowing through crowd, daggers at belt, cornered predator eyes, desperate aggressive expression, overcast daylight, street crowd, taller people towering around, aggressive energy, gothic, dark fantasy, vertical panel`\n**Priority**: LOW\n\n---\n\n## Panel 34 -- Recruit Yard Waiting\n**Current prompt summary**: `wide shot, medieval military training yard, mixed group of recruits standing in loose clusters, diverse fantasy races, u...`\n**Issues**:\n- Canon lock says: \"Per Brigit Ch 5: 'He's why we don't remember signing up.' A subtle 'wait, when did I sign up for this?' reaction.\" This is a deliberate canon plant that the current prompt does not include. One recruit should look confused/disoriented rather than merely uncomfortable.\n**Refined prompt**: `masterpiece, best quality, absurdres, wide shot, medieval military training yard, mixed group of recruits standing in loose clusters, diverse fantasy races, uncomfortable silence, one recruit looking confused and disoriented as if they cannot remember arriving, wooden practice dummies, scarlet banners, stone walls, overcast sky, awkward energy, gothic, dark fantasy`\n**Priority**: MEDIUM\n\n---\n\n## Panel 37 -- Triangle Management Diagram\n**Current prompt summary**: `1boy, gnome, tiny, immaculate suit, wooden pointer, gesturing at diagram board, triangle diagram with text, two half-og...`\n**Issues**:\n- The prompt text says the triangle diagram has \"FINKLE\" at top and \"YOU LOT\" at bottom with \"TAXES\" in the middle. Skips upscale by default.\n     6→Handles ComfyUI going down (WSL2 ~90s restart cycle) with retry logic.\n     7→\n     8→Usage:\n     9→  python3 generate-batch.py                     # generate all missing panels\n    10→  python3 generate-batch.py --test              # test batch (5 representative panels)\n    11→  python3 generate-batch.py --panel 25          # single panel\n    12→  python3 generate-batch.py --dry-run           # show what would be generated\n    13→  python3 generate-batch.py --with-upscale      # enable 4x upscale (slow, needs VRAM)\n    14→\"\"\"\n    15→\n    16→import json\n    17→import urllib.request\n    18→import urllib.error\n    19→import uuid\n    20→import time\n    21→import os\n    22→import argparse\n    23→import sys\n    24→\n    25→COMFYUI_URL = \"http://192.168.0.121:8188\"\n    26→CHECKPOINT = \"NoobAI-XL-v1.1.safetensors\"\n    27→STYLE_LORA = \"iLLMythD4rkL1nesV2.safetensors\"\n    28→STYLE_LORA_WEIGHT = 0.7\n    29→STYLE_TRIGGER = \"D4rkL1nes\"\n    30→UPSCALE_MODEL = \"4x-UltraSharp.pth\"\n    31→FACE_DETECTOR = \"bbox/face_yolov8m.pt\"\n    32→OUTPUT_DIR = \"/Volumes/synologyNFS/server-manager/projects/mardov/assets/pilots/pilot-a/panels\"\n    33→JOBS_FILE = \"/Volumes/synologyNFS/server-manager/projects/mardov/production/panel-jobs.json\"\n    34→\n    35→# Lora name fixups: panel-jobs.json may reference old names\n    36→LORA_FIXUPS = {\n    37→    \"brigit.safetensors\": \"brigit-old.safetensors\",\n    38→}\n    39→\n    40→WIDTH = 832\n    41→HEIGHT = 1216\n    42→STEPS = 25\n    43→CFG = 7.0\n    44→SAMPLER = \"euler_ancestral\"\n    45→SCHEDULER = \"karras\"\n    46→SEED_BASE = 44000  # New seed base for this batch\n    47→\n    48→NEGATIVE_PROMPT = (\n    49→    \"(text:1.5), (english text:1.5), (writing:1.4), (letters:1.4), (words:1.4), \"\n    50→    \"(caption:1.3), (subtitle:1.3), (title:1.3), (speech bubble:1.3), (logo:1.3), \"\n    51→    \"(watermark:1.4), (signature:1.4), (number:1.2), (symbol:1.2), \"\n    52→    \"blurry, low quality, deformed, extra fingers, \"\n    53→    \"bad anatomy, poorly drawn, amateur, photorealistic, 3d render, photograph, \"\n    54→    \"ugly, bad hands, mutated hands, mutation, extra limbs, missing arms, \"\n    55→    \"missing legs, bad proportions, gross proportions, malformed limbs, \"\n    56→    \"fused fingers, too many fingers, long neck, cropped, worst quality, \"\n    57→    \"normal quality, username, artist name, jpeg artifacts\"\n    58→)\n    59→\n    60→# Test panels: 1 establishing, 1 character LoRA, 1 action, 1 atmospheric, 1 credits\n    61→TEST_PANELS = [7, 25, 44, 55, 77]\n    62→\n    63→\n    64→def build_workflow(prompt_text, seed=42, filename_prefix=\"panel\",\n    65→                   use_upscale=False, char_lora_files=None):\n    66→    \"\"\"Build ComfyUI workflow with VAEDecodeTiled for VRAM safety.\"\"\"\n    67→    full_prompt = f\"{STYLE_TRIGGER}, no text, text free, {prompt_text}\"\n    68→\n    69→    workflow = {}\n    70→\n    71→    # Node 1: Load checkpoint\n    72→    workflow[\"1\"] = {\n    73→        \"class_type\": \"CheckpointLoaderSimple\",\n    74→        \"inputs\": {\"ckpt_name\": CHECKPOINT}\n    75→    }\n    76→\n    77→    # Node 10: Style LoRA\n    78→    workflow[\"10\"] = {\n    79→        \"class_type\": \"LoraLoader\",\n    80→        \"inputs\": {\n    81→            \"lora_name\": STYLE_LORA,\n    82→            \"strength_model\": STYLE_LORA_WEIGHT,\n    83→            \"strength_clip\": STYLE_LORA_WEIGHT,\n    84→            \"model\": [\"1\", 0],\n    85→            \"clip\": [\"1\", 1]\n    86→        }\n    87→    }\n    88→\n    89→    # Chain character LoRAs\n    90→    last_lora_node = \"10\"\n    91→    if char_lora_files:\n    92→        for idx, lora_file in enumerate(char_lora_files):\n    93→            # Apply fixups for renamed LoRAs\n    94→            lora_file = LORA_FIXUPS.get(lora_file, lora_file)\n    95→            node_id = str(11 + idx)\n    96→            workflow[node_id] = {\n    97→                \"class_type\": \"LoraLoader\",\n    98→                \"inputs\": {\n    99→                    \"lora_name\": lora_file,\n   100→                    \"strength_model\": 0.55,\n   101→                    \"strength_clip\": 0.55,\n   102→                    \"model\": [last_lora_node, 0],\n   103→                    \"clip\": [last_lora_node, 1]\n   104→                }\n   105→            }\n   106→            last_lora_node = node_id\n   107→\n   108→    # Node 3: Positive prompt\n   109→    workflow[\"3\"] = {\n   110→        \"class_type\": \"CLIPTextEncode\",\n   111→        \"inputs\": {\"text\": full_prompt, \"clip\": [last_lora_node, 1]}\n   112→    }\n   113→\n   114→    # Node 4: Negative prompt\n   115→    workflow[\"4\"] = {\n   116→        \"class_type\": \"CLIPTextEncode\",\n   117→        \"inputs\": {\"text\": NEGATIVE_PROMPT, \"clip\": [last_lora_node, 1]}\n   118→    }\n   119→\n   120→    # Node 5: Empty latent\n   121→    workflow[\"5\"] = {\n   122→        \"class_type\": \"EmptyLatentImage\",\n   123→        \"inputs\": {\"width\": WIDTH, \"height\": HEIGHT, \"batch_size\": 1}\n   124→    }\n   125→\n   126→    # Node 6: KSampler\n   127→    workflow[\"6\"] = {\n   128→        \"class_type\": \"KSampler\",\n   129→        \"inputs\": {\n   130→            \"model\": [last_lora_node, 0],\n   131→            \"positive\": [\"3\", 0],\n   132→            \"negative\": [\"4\", 0],\n   133→            \"latent_image\": [\"5\", 0],\n   134→            \"seed\": seed,\n   135→            \"steps\": STEPS,\n   136→            \"cfg\": CFG,\n   137→            \"sampler_name\": SAMPLER,\n   138→            \"scheduler\": SCHEDULER,\n   139→            \"denoise\": 1.0\n   140→        }\n   141→    }\n   142→\n   143→    # Node 7: VAEDecode\n   144→    # Note: VAEDecodeTiled requires overlap/temporal params in ComfyUI 0.15.1.\n   145→    # Regular VAEDecode works at 832x1216 with --lowvram --force-fp16 (tested).\n   146→    workflow[\"7\"] = {\n   147→        \"class_type\": \"VAEDecode\",\n   148→        \"inputs\": {\n   149→            \"samples\": [\"6\", 0],\n   150→            \"vae\": [\"1\", 2],\n   151→        }\n   152→    }\n   153→\n   154→    current_image_node = \"7\"\n   155→\n   156→    # Upscale (optional -- heavy on VRAM)\n   157→    if use_upscale:\n   158→        workflow[\"30\"] = {\n   159→            \"class_type\": \"UpscaleModelLoader\",\n   160→            \"inputs\": {\"model_name\": UPSCALE_MODEL}\n   161→        }\n   162→        workflow[\"31\"] = {\n   163→            \"class_type\": \"ImageUpscaleWithModel\",\n   164→            \"inputs\": {\"upscale_model\": [\"30\", 0], \"image\": [current_image_node, 0]}\n   165→        }\n   166→        workflow[\"32\"] = {\n   167→            \"class_type\": \"ImageScaleBy\",\n   168→            \"inputs\": {\"image\": [\"31\", 0], \"upscale_method\": \"lanczos\", \"scale_by\": 0.5}\n   169→        }\n   170→        current_image_node = \"32\"\n   171→\n   172→    # Node 40: Save image\n   173→    workflow[\"40\"] = {\n   174→        \"class_type\": \"SaveImage\",\n   175→        \"inputs\": {\n   176→            \"images\": [current_image_node, 0],\n   177→            \"filename_prefix\": filename_prefix\n   178→        }\n   179→    }\n   180→\n   181→    return workflow\n   182→\n   183→\n   184→def queue_prompt(workflow, client_id=None):\n   185→    if client_id is None:\n   186→        client_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n   187→    payload = json.dumps({\"prompt\": workflow, \"client_id\": client_id}).encode()\n   188→    req = urllib.request.Request(\n   189→        f\"{COMFYUI_URL}/prompt\", data=payload,\n   190→        headers={\"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"}\n   191→    )\n   192→    try:\n   193→        resp = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)\n   194→        result = json.loads(resp.read())\n   195→        return result.get(\"prompt_id\")\n   196→    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n   197→        body = e.read().decode()[:500]\n   198→        print(f\"  ERROR: HTTP {e.code} - {body}\")\n   199→        return None\n   200→    except Exception as e:"}]},"uuid":"d3adfe39-9922-4d97-8a80-c7fe8029c8a6","timestamp":"2026-03-18T03:08:50.795Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"20dd5882-4df4-4378-8324-3c8df67037ef","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"b2830bc4-20b6-448a-894f-acac9cd80ccc","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"00d91d86-ad69-404a-aad8-2a048e6b309c","agentId":"a47eb08dc424f0771","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016byU1ML3MS8rBWNYhLZmwP","type":"tool_result","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Panel generator with IP-Adapter for character consistency.\n\nUses IP-Adapter + character LoRA + style LoRA for consistent character appearance.\nPipeline: NoobAI-XL → Style LoRA → Char LoRA → IP-Adapter → KSampler → FaceDetailer → Upscale\n\nUsage: python3 generate-panels-ipadapter.py [--dry-run] [--panel N] [--no-upscale]\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport urllib.request\nimport urllib.error\nimport uuid\nimport time\nimport os\nimport argparse\n\nCOMFYUI_URL = \"http://192.168.0.121:8188\"\nCHECKPOINT = \"NoobAI-XL-v1.1.safetensors\"\nSTYLE_LORA = \"iLLMythD4rkL1nesV2.safetensors\"\nSTYLE_LORA_WEIGHT = 0.4  # Lower when IP-Adapter is active\nSTYLE_TRIGGER = \"D4rkL1nes\"\nIPADAPTER_MODEL = \"ip-adapter-plus_sdxl_vit-h.safetensors\"\nCLIP_VISION_MODEL = \"CLIP-ViT-H-14-laion2B.safetensors\"\nIPADAPTER_WEIGHT = 0.55\nCHAR_LORA_WEIGHT = 0.55  # Lower when IP-Adapter supplements\nUPSCALE_MODEL = \"4x-UltraSharp.pth\"\nFACE_DETECTOR = \"bbox/face_yolov8m.pt\"\nOUTPUT_DIR = \"/Volumes/synologyNFS/server-manager/projects/mardov/assets/pilots/pilot-a/panels-v3\"\nJOBS_FILE = \"/Volumes/synologyNFS/server-manager/projects/mardov/production/panel-jobs.json\"\nREF_DIR = \"/Volumes/synologyNFS/server-manager/projects/mardov/production/character-refs\"\n\n# Character reference image mapping (LoRA name -> reference image file)\nCHAR_REF_IMAGES = {\n    \"sprocket\": \"sprocket-ref.png\",\n    \"nyx\": \"nyx-ref.png\",\n    \"stalwart-little\": \"stalwart-little-ref.png\",\n    \"brigit-new\": \"brigit-ref.png\",\n    \"varis\": \"varis-ref.png\",\n    \"daina\": \"daina-ref-mj.png\",\n    \"hal\": \"hal-ref-mj.png\",\n    \"nocturnus\": \"nocturnus-ref-mj.png\",\n}\n\nWIDTH = 832\nHEIGHT = 1216\nSTEPS = 25\nCFG = 7.0\nSAMPLER = \"euler_ancestral\"\nSCHEDULER = \"karras\"\nSEED_BASE = 43000  # New seed base for v3\n\nNEGATIVE_PROMPT = (\n    \"(text:1.5), (english text:1.5), (writing:1.4), (letters:1.4), (words:1.4), \"\n    \"(caption:1.3), (subtitle:1.3), (title:1.3), (speech bubble:1.3), (logo:1.3), \"\n    \"(watermark:1.4), (signature:1.4), (number:1.2), (symbol:1.2), \"\n    \"blurry, low quality, deformed, extra fingers, \"\n    \"bad anatomy, poorly drawn, amateur, photorealistic, 3d render, photograph, \"\n    \"ugly, bad hands, mutated hands, mutation, extra limbs, missing arms, \"\n    \"missing legs, bad proportions, gross proportions, malformed limbs, \"\n    \"fused fingers, too many fingers, long neck, cropped, worst quality, \"\n    \"normal quality, username, artist name, jpeg artifacts\"\n)\n\nTEXT_PANELS = {76, 80}\n\n\ndef upload_image(filepath):\n    \"\"\"Upload reference image to ComfyUI and return the filename.\"\"\"\n    import mimetypes\n    filename = os.path.basename(filepath)\n\n    # Check if already uploaded\n    try:\n        resp = urllib.request.urlopen(f\"{COMFYUI_URL}/view?filename={filename}&type=input\", timeout=5)\n        if resp.status == 200:\n            return filename\n    except:\n        pass\n\n    # Upload\n    boundary = uuid.uuid4().hex\n    content_type = mimetypes.guess_type(filepath)[0] or 'image/png'\n\n    with open(filepath, 'rb') as f:\n        file_data = f.read()\n\n    body = (\n        f'--{boundary}\\r\\n'\n        f'Content-Disposition: form-data; name=\"image\"; filename=\"{filename}\"\\r\\n'\n        f'Content-Type: {content_type}\\r\\n\\r\\n'\n    ).encode() + file_data + f'\\r\\n--{boundary}--\\r\\n'.encode()\n\n    req = urllib.request.Request(\n        f\"{COMFYUI_URL}/upload/image\",\n        data=body,\n        headers={'Content-Type': f'multipart/form-data; boundary={boundary}'}\n    )\n    try:\n        resp = urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30)\n        result = json.loads(resp.read())\n        return result.get(\"name\", filename)\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"  Upload error for {filename}: {e}\")\n        return None\n\n\ndef build_workflow(prompt_text, seed=42, filename_prefix=\"panel\",\n                   use_face_detailer=True, use_upscale=True,\n                   char_lora_files=None, ref_image_name=None, no_text=True):\n    \"\"\"Build IP-Adapter + LoRA ComfyUI pipeline.\"\"\"\n    if no_text:\n        full_prompt = f\"{STYLE_TRIGGER}, no text, text free, {prompt_text}\"\n    else:\n        full_prompt = f\"{STYLE_TRIGGER}, {prompt_text}\"\n\n    workflow = {}\n\n    # Node 1: Load checkpoint\n    workflow[\"1\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"CheckpointLoaderSimple\",\n        \"inputs\": {\"ckpt_name\": CHECKPOINT}\n    }\n\n    # Node 10: Load style LoRA\n    workflow[\"10\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"LoraLoader\",\n        \"inputs\": {\n            \"lora_name\": STYLE_LORA,\n            \"strength_model\": STYLE_LORA_WEIGHT,\n            \"strength_clip\": STYLE_LORA_WEIGHT,\n            \"model\": [\"1\", 0],\n            \"clip\": [\"1\", 1]\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Chain character LoRAs after style LoRA\n    last_lora_node = \"10\"\n    if char_lora_files:\n        for idx, lora_file in enumerate(char_lora_files):\n            node_id = str(11 + idx)\n            workflow[node_id] = {\n                \"class_type\": \"LoraLoader\",\n                \"inputs\": {\n                    \"lora_name\": lora_file,\n                    \"strength_model\": CHAR_LORA_WEIGHT,\n                    \"strength_clip\": CHAR_LORA_WEIGHT,\n                    \"model\": [last_lora_node, 0],\n                    \"clip\": [last_lora_node, 1]\n                }\n            }\n            last_lora_node = node_id\n\n    # IP-Adapter nodes (if reference image provided)\n    model_node = last_lora_node\n    if ref_image_name:\n        # Node 50: Load IP-Adapter model\n        workflow[\"50\"] = {\n            \"class_type\": \"IPAdapterModelLoader\",\n            \"inputs\": {\"ipadapter_file\": IPADAPTER_MODEL}\n        }\n\n        # Node 51: Load CLIP Vision\n        workflow[\"51\"] = {\n            \"class_type\": \"CLIPVisionLoader\",\n            \"inputs\": {\"clip_name\": CLIP_VISION_MODEL}\n        }\n\n        # Node 52: Load reference image\n        workflow[\"52\"] = {\n            \"class_type\": \"LoadImage\",\n            \"inputs\": {\"image\": ref_image_name}\n        }\n\n        # Node 53: Apply IP-Adapter\n        workflow[\"53\"] = {\n            \"class_type\": \"IPAdapterAdvanced\",\n            \"inputs\": {\n                \"model\": [last_lora_node, 0],\n                \"ipadapter\": [\"50\", 0],\n                \"clip_vision\": [\"51\", 0],\n                \"image\": [\"52\", 0],\n                \"weight\": IPADAPTER_WEIGHT,\n                \"weight_type\": \"linear\",\n                \"combine_embeds\": \"concat\",\n                \"start_at\": 0.0,\n                \"end_at\": 0.85,\n                \"embeds_scaling\": \"V only\",\n            }\n        }\n        model_node = \"53\"\n\n    # Node 3: Positive prompt\n    workflow[\"3\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"CLIPTextEncode\",\n        \"inputs\": {\"text\": full_prompt, \"clip\": [last_lora_node, 1]}\n    }\n\n    # Node 4: Negative prompt\n    workflow[\"4\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"CLIPTextEncode\",\n        \"inputs\": {\"text\": NEGATIVE_PROMPT, \"clip\": [last_lora_node, 1]}\n    }\n\n    # Node 5: Empty latent\n    workflow[\"5\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"EmptyLatentImage\",\n        \"inputs\": {\"width\": WIDTH, \"height\": HEIGHT, \"batch_size\": 1}\n    }\n\n    # Node 6: KSampler\n    workflow[\"6\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"KSampler\",\n        \"inputs\": {\n            \"model\": [model_node, 0],\n            \"positive\": [\"3\", 0],\n            \"negative\": [\"4\", 0],\n            \"latent_image\": [\"5\", 0],\n            \"seed\": seed,\n            \"steps\": STEPS,\n            \"cfg\": CFG,\n            \"sampler_name\": SAMPLER,\n            \"scheduler\": SCHEDULER,\n            \"denoise\": 1.0\n        }\n    }\n\n    # Node 7: VAEDecode\n    workflow[\"7\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"VAEDecode\",\n        \"inputs\": {\"samples\": [\"6\", 0], \"vae\": [\"1\", 2]}\n    }\n\n    current_image_node = \"7\"\n\n    # FaceDetailer (optional)\n    if use_face_detailer:\n        workflow[\"20\"] = {\n            \"class_type\": \"UltralyticsDetectorProvider\",\n            \"inputs\": {\"model_name\": FACE_DETECTOR}\n        }\n        workflow[\"21\"] = {\n            \"class_type\": \"FaceDetailer\",\n            \"inputs\": {\n                \"image\": [current_image_node, 0],\n                \"model\": [model_node, 0],\n                \"clip\": [last_lora_node, 1],\n                \"vae\": [\"1\", 2],\n                \"positive\": [\"3\", 0],\n                \"negative\": [\"4\", 0],\n                \"bbox_detector\": [\"20\", 0],\n                \"seed\": seed, \"steps\": 20, \"cfg\": CFG,\n                \"sampler_name\": SAMPLER, \"scheduler\": SCHEDULER,\n                \"denoise\": 0.4, \"guide_size\": 512, \"guide_size_for\": True,\n                \"max_size\": 1024, \"feather\": 5, \"noise_mask\": True,\n                \"force_inpaint\": True, \"wildcard\": \"\", \"cycle\": 1,\n                \"inpaint_model\": False, \"noise_mask_feather\": 20,\n                \"sam_model_opt\": None, \"segm_detector_opt\": None,\n                \"detailer_hook\": None, \"bbox_threshold\": 0.5,\n                \"bbox_dilation\": 10, \"bbox_crop_factor\": 3.0,\n                \"sam_detection_hint\": \"center-1\", \"sam_dilation\": 0,\n                \"sam_threshold\": 0.93, \"sam_bbox_expansion\": 0,\n                \"sam_mask_hint_threshold\": 0.7,\n                \"sam_mask_hint_use_negative\": \"False\", \"drop_size\": 10,\n            }\n        }\n        current_image_node = \"21\"\n\n    # Upscale (optional)\n    if use_upscale:\n        workflow[\"30\"] = {\n            \"class_type\": \"UpscaleModelLoader\",\n            \"inputs\": {\"model_name\": UPSCALE_MODEL}\n        }\n        workflow[\"31\"] = {\n            \"class_type\": \"ImageUpscaleWithModel\",\n            \"inputs\": {\"upscale_model\": [\"30\", 0], \"image\": [current_image_node, 0]}\n        }\n        workflow[\"32\"] = {\n            \"class_type\": \"ImageScaleBy\",\n            \"inputs\": {\"image\": [\"31\", 0], \"upscale_method\": \"lanczos\", \"scale_by\": 0.5}\n        }\n        current_image_node = \"32\"\n\n    # Save\n    workflow[\"40\"] = {\n        \"class_type\": \"SaveImage\",\n        \"inputs\": {\"images\": [current_image_node, 0], \"filename_prefix\": filename_prefix}\n    }\n\n    return workflow\n\n\ndef queue_prompt(workflow, client_id=None):\n    if client_id is None:\n        client_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n    payload = json.dumps({\"prompt\": workflow, \"client_id\": client_id}).encode()\n    req = urllib.request.Request(\n        f\"{COMFYUI_URL}/prompt\", data=payload,\n        headers={\"Content-Type\": \"application/json\"}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"1f80e16b-d9a4-440f-8025-9669a8451f8b","timestamp":"2026-03-18T03:08:51.000Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b2830bc4-20b6-448a-894f-acac9cd80ccc","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/eviemini/Documents/code/server-manager","sessionId":"a5125120-9cbe-408a-953a-457fed0eba08","version":"2.1.78","gitBranch":"main","slug":"fluffy-cuddling-tome"}

{"parentUuid":"c5afb155-a3d5-4ba3-b475-ad305550d9bb","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"00d91d86-ad69-404a-aad8-2a048e6b309c","agentId":"a47eb08dc424f0771","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CCKpiZa94LnTmwvHHSeLbK","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→# Mardov Pilot A -- Story Bible\n     2→\n     3→**Series**: Heroes of Mardov\n     4→**Format**: Gothic anime motion webtoon (vertical scroll)\n     5→**Pilot Length**: 80 panels across 7 acts\n     6→**Visual Style**: Dark fantasy, manhwa ink lines, Illustrious XL 2.0 + gothic LoRA stack\n     7→**Aspect Ratio**: 832x1216 (2:3 webtoon tall)\n     8→\n     9→---\n    10→\n    11→## Logline\n    12→\n    13→When a band of strangers answers a military summons to a besieged port town, they are led into an ancient dungeon beneath the streets -- and into a betrayal that reveals a sealed door bearing the crest of the Raven Queen, a god who should not have business with the living.\n    14→\n    15→---\n    16→\n    17→## Synopsis\n    18→\n    19→On a windswept coastal cliff, Conquis Abbey receives an urgent military summons. Riders depart at dawn, traveling through a darkening landscape scarred by something predatory, and arrive at a port town bracing for a threat it cannot name. Within the town, a secret forge produces a weapon that burns with unnatural light -- a god-killer blade whose very existence hints at divine stakes the townspeople cannot comprehend.\n    20→\n    21→As dusk settles, we meet the adventurers who will form our party: Sprocket, a gnome diviner reading fate in his cramped carriage study; Brigit, a druid who shifts from wolf to woman at the town walls; Stalwart, a golden-scaled dragonborn paladin who commands attention on every street; and Nyx, a tiefling warlock scribbling secrets in a candlelit inn room. Three more figures flash past in quick cuts -- a wild-eyed mage losing control of her own magic, a furiously short man elbowing through crowds, and a gnome with clever hands and too many pockets. All roads lead to the same recruitment yard.\n    22→\n    23→There, a tyrannical gnome bureaucrat named Finkle -- three feet of immaculate suit and pure disdain -- takes command of the recruits with a triangle diagram placing himself firmly at the top. The awkward strangers spar, disperse, and reconvene at a tavern, where fragile camaraderie is shattered by the arrival of Jorgen, a bleeding ranger who crashes through the door with kobolds on his heels. After a vicious tavern brawl, a cryptic note draws the party underground through hidden sewer passages into architecture older than civilization itself. They face a kobold standoff in the dark, navigate trapped corridors, and survive a descending ceiling carved with a single word: KNEEL.\n    24→\n    25→At the bottom of a spiral staircase, crimson light replaces torchlight. They enter a cathedral-scale ritual chamber dominated by four supernatural waterfalls -- blood-red, acid-green, mercury-orange, and soul-blue -- cascading into stone basins around a central altar. This is the pilot's full-page revelation: a space that should not exist beneath a port town, radiating power that predates human memory. Then Jorgen, the ranger who led them here, turns his weapon on the party. In the chaos that follows, they discover a massive sealed steel door bearing the crest of the Raven Queen -- concentric rings of forged metal inscribed in a language older than the common tongue. They stand before it, battered, betrayed, and confronted by a question none of them came here to answer: what lies behind the door, and why does a death goddess need it sealed?\n    26→\n    27→---\n    28→\n    29→## Act Structure\n    30→\n    31→### Act 1: The Call (Panels 1-7)\n    32→\n    33→**Setting**: Conquis Abbey, a crumbling gothic monastery on a coastal cliff.\n    34→**Tone**: Ancient weight, institutional weariness, gathering dread.\n    35→**Color Temperature**: Cool greys, storm-light, candlelit amber interiors.\n    36→\n    37→The pilot opens with absolute silence and scale. Conquis Abbey is introduced as a place older than the conflict it now serves -- beautiful and broken, its flying buttresses cracked by salt wind, its gargoyles eroded into ghosts of their original forms. Ravens circle the bell tower. The sea churns below. This is not a place of adventure. This is a place that has seen too much.\n    38→\n    39→Inside, the great hall has been repurposed as a war room. Maps and military correspondence cover every surface. Half-eaten meals suggest people have not left this room in days. A rain-soaked messenger arrives bearing a sealed scroll -- the wax seal is military, official, the kind that means mobilization. An officer reads the summons aloud to grim faces. The camera moves to the war table: a top-down map view with fresh red markers showing threat positions, and a single circled destination -- the port town.\n    40→\n    41→Night falls. The abbey goes dark except for one amber window where someone is still deciding. This is the last quiet moment before movement begins.\n    42→\n    43→**Panel Breakdown**:\n    44→\n    45→| Panel | Title | Description | Characters | Mood | Camera |\n    46→|-------|-------|-------------|------------|------|--------|\n    47→| 1 | Conquis Abbey | Wide establishing shot. Ancient gothic abbey on a coastal cliff above grey churning sea. Crumbling buttresses, faintly glowing stained glass, circling ravens, ocean mist. No humans visible. | None | Ancient weight, isolation | Wide establishing, slightly low angle |\n    48→| 2 | Abbey Interior -- War Room | Interior of the great hall. Vaulted stone ceiling, long oak table covered in maps and correspondence, candelabras with melting candles, military banners in scarlet and gold, dusty stained-glass light. | None (implied figures) | Institutional tension | Mid-wide interior, eye level |\n    49→| 3 | The Summons | A rain-soaked messenger kneels at the threshold, mud-covered, cloak torn from hard riding. He holds out a sealed scroll with a prominent wax seal. | Messenger | Urgency, exhaustion | Mid-shot, slightly above |\n    50→| 4 | The Seal | Extreme close-up of the red wax seal on the scroll. Official military crest -- mobilization, not correspondence. Travel-worn parchment, water stains. | None (object focus) | Dread, authority | Extreme close-up, macro |\n    51→| 5 | The Order | An officer reads the scroll aloud. Grim, controlled expression. The assembled room listens with faces that have heard too much bad news to react visibly. | Officer, war room assembly | Controlled despair | Mid-shot, eye level |\n    52→| 6 | The Map | Top-down view of the war table. Aged fantasy map of coastal region, faded ink roads and town symbols. Fresh red markers showing threats, force positions, and a single circled destination. | None (map focus) | Strategic clarity | Overhead/bird's eye |\n    53→| 7 | Abbey Night -- Decision | Exterior, night. The abbey is mostly dark but one window glows amber. Moonlight on ocean waves below. A solitary figure visible as a shadow in the lit window. | Solitary figure (silhouette) | Solitude, resolve | Wide exterior, mid-distance |\n    54→\n    55→---\n    56→\n    57→### Act 2: The Journey (Panels 8-14)\n    58→\n    59→**Setting**: The road from Conquis Abbey to the port town.\n    60→**Tone**: Cinematic travel, environmental storytelling, escalating unease.\n    61→**Color Temperature**: Pale dawn transitioning to dark forest greens and greys, ending in overcast harbor light.\n    62→\n    63→Dawn breaks. Riders in scarlet cloaks depart through the abbey gates, silhouetted against pale sunrise. The road sequence is a landscape poem: winding through dark autumn forests, across old stone bridges over misty streams. The world is beautiful and wounded. An abandoned merchant cart with deep claw marks -- no bodies, which is worse than bodies -- tells the audience everything about what waits ahead without a word of dialogue.\n    64→\n    65→The port town appears from a hillside: stone buildings around a natural harbor, smoke from chimneys, a handful of ships at anchor. It looks safe from a distance. At the gate, a queue of merchants and refugees tells a different story. Inside, citizens board windows, carry water barrels, and drill in courtyards. A notice board covered in recruitment posters and missing persons reports is the first direct storytelling panel -- the audience can read the town's fear in its own words.\n    66→\n    67→**Panel Breakdown**:\n    68→\n    69→| Panel | Title | Description | Characters | Mood | Camera |\n    70→|-------|-------|-------------|------------|------|--------|\n    71→| 8 | Dawn Riders Depart | Dawn. Riders on horseback through abbey gates, silhouetted against pale sunrise. Scarlet cloaks streaming, horses' breath visible in cold air. Dusty road ahead through dark landscape. | Riders (silhouette) | Purpose, departure | Wide, backlit |\n    72→| 9 | The Road | Wide landscape. Riders small on a winding road through dark forest. Autumn leaves drifting, old stone bridge, misty stream. Beautiful and foreboding. | Riders (distant) | Pastoral unease | Ultra-wide landscape |\n    73→| 10 | Roadside Discovery | Abandoned merchant cart overturned at roadside. Goods scattered, horse gone, deep claw marks in the wood. No bodies. Riders have stopped to examine it. | Riders examining | Quiet horror | Mid-wide, eye level |\n    74→| 11 | Port Town -- First Sight | Wide establishing from hillside. Port town below: stone buildings, natural harbor, wooden docks, ships at anchor, chimney smoke. Looks peaceful from distance. | None (landscape) | Deceptive calm | Wide establishing, elevated |\n    75→| 12 | Town Gate | Stone archway with iron portcullis, guards in mismatched armor. Queue of merchants and refugees. The town receives more people than usual. | Guards, travelers | Pressure, anxiety | Mid-wide, ground level |\n    76→| 13 | Town Preparations | Citizens prepare for threat. Shopkeeper boards windows. Men carry water barrels. Background: soldiers drill in loose formation. | Townspeople | Communal dread | Mid-wide, street level |\n    77→| 14 | Notice Board | Close-up of town notice board on stone wall. Overlapping parchments: trade notices, missing persons, road warnings. One fresh recruitment poster stands out, prominent and official. | None (environmental) | Information density | Close-up, slightly angled |\n    78→\n    79→---\n    80→\n    81→### Act 3: The Forge (Panels 15-20)\n    82→\n    83→**Setting**: A blacksmith's forge in a narrow cobblestone alley; port town at dusk.\n    84→**Tone**: Sacred craft, supernatural undercurrent, mythic weight.\n    85→**Color Temperature**: Intense orange forge-glow against dark stone, transitioning to golden dusk amber.\n    86→\n    87→This is the first overtly supernatural sequence. In a narrow alley, a forge pours smoke and sparks. Inside, a smith hammers glowing metal on an anvil etched with arcane runes -- runes that should not be on a blacksmith's tool. The metal glows with colors that are not natural: blue-gold energy that suggests the blade being forged is not merely a weapon but something that answers to a higher authority.\n    88→\n    89→The quenching scene is the act's centerpiece: when the blade hits water, the steam carries light -- crackling blue-gold energy along the edge. This is not metallurgy. This is ritual. The completed weapon rests on dark velvet, carrying a faint supernatural glow and arcane inscriptions that appear etched by light rather than by chisel.\n    90→\n    91→The act climaxes with the smith raising the finished god-killer overhead, its light filling the entire forge. Then a quiet exhale: the town at dusk, golden and peaceful, unaware of what was just forged in its belly.\n    92→\n    93→**Panel Breakdown**:\n    94→\n    95→| Panel | Title | Description | Characters | Mood | Camera |\n    96→|-------|-------|-------------|------------|------|--------|\n    97→| 15 | The Forge -- Exterior | Blacksmith's forge in a narrow cobblestone alley. Smoke and sparks from chimney. Intense orange glow leaks from ajar wooden door. Iron tools on exterior walls. | None | Mystery, heat | Mid establishing, street level |\n    98→| 16 | The Forge -- Interior | Muscular smith hammers glowing metal on a rune-etched anvil. The metal glows unnatural colors. Forge heat distorts the air. Arcane runes on the anvil pulse faintly. | Smith | Sacred labor | Mid-shot, slightly low angle |\n    99→| 17 | The Blade | Extreme close-up: blade quenched in water. The steam explosion carries blue-gold light crackling along the edge. Supernatural energy, not normal metallurgy. Water surface reflects the glow. | Smith's hands | Awe, power | Extreme close-up |\n   100→| 18 | The Weapon Complete | Completed weapon on dark velvet cloth. Forge embers dying behind it. Single candle illuminates the blade's faint blue-gold glow and arcane inscriptions etched by light. | None (weapon focus) | Reverence, stillness | Close-up, overhead tilt |\n   101→| 19 | Forging God-Killer | Wide dramatic shot. Smith raises finished weapon overhead, blue-gold light illuminating the entire forge. Sharp shadows. Sparks cascade. The moment of mythic completion. | Smith | Triumph, mythic scale | Wide, low angle |\n   102→| 20 | Town at Dusk | Wide establishing. Port town at golden sunset. Amber light on stone buildings, long shadows on cobblestone. Ships quiet in harbor. Lanterns being lit. The town does not know what was just made. | Townspeople (distant) | False peace, golden hour | Wide establishing, elevated |\n   103→\n   104→---\n   105→\n   106→### Act 4: The Characters (Panels 21-44)\n   107→\n   108→**Setting**: Various locations across the port town -- carriages, forest edges, docks, inn rooms, the recruitment yard, a tavern.\n   109→**Tone**: Intimate vignettes building to ensemble comedy, shattered by violence.\n   110→**Color Temperature**: Warm interior golds, cool exterior greens and greys, shifting to warm tavern amber.\n   111→\n   112→This is the longest act and the emotional core of the pilot. Each main character receives a two-panel vignette that establishes who they are before they meet each other. The vignettes are carefully structured: an interior/private moment showing the character's true self, followed by a public/exterior moment showing how the world sees them.\n   113→\n   114→**Sprocket** (Panels 21-22): A gnome diviner, ~3.5 feet tall, warm tan skin, golden-yellow spiked hair, large dark analytical eyes, gap-toothed grin, bushy sideburns, brass goggles. We first see him in a cramped moving carriage study surrounded by books and astronomical instruments, calculating star charts with feverish concentration -- his hands stained with ink, half-eaten sandwich forgotten. Then he arrives at the port town, small among the crowd, grinning with the expression of someone who knows something is about to happen.\n   115→\n   116→**Brigit** (Panels 23-26): A young human woman druid. Dark auburn hair tangled with leaves and twigs, pale porcelain skin, green eyes. We meet her first as a grey-brown wolf at the edge of a scarred, recovering forest -- alert, poised, wild. Then the shift: mid-transformation from wolf to human at the town wall, liquid and organic, fur dissolving into freckled skin. She walks barefoot through town, scanning everything like a predator in an unfamiliar ecosystem. At the notice board, she touches the recruitment poster with recognition -- not mercenary calculation, but a druid sensing something wrong that she cannot leave alone.\n   117→\n   118→**Stalwart** (Panels 27-28): A dragonborn paladin, seven feet tall, golden-yellow reptilian scales, spiky golden mane, dark amber eyes, heavy dark metallic armor with a scarlet surcoat. Dawn at the dock: sunrise catches his scales as he steps off a ship, and the dock workers stop to stare. Walking through town, he takes up most of the road. Citizens react with awe, wariness, stepping aside. He carries himself with weight and purpose -- a walking altar.\n   119→\n   120→**Nyx** (Panels 29-30): A young tiefling warlock, petite build, pale pinkish-lavender skin, small curved horns, white/pale blonde voluminous curly hair. Candlelight in a modest inn room: she sits cross-legged on a bed, writing furiously in a leather journal, cobalt-blue cloak pooled around her. Then stepping through the inn doorway into morning light, pulling the cloak tight, journal tucked against her chest -- the posture of someone leaving something behind.\n   121→\n   122→**Quick Cuts** (Panel 31a-c): Three vertical panels in rapid succession. A wild-eyed human woman in ill-fitting mage robes with uncontrolled magic sparking from her fingertips -- hot white-gold involuntary sparks, her expression equal parts terror and exhilaration. A 4-foot-tall adult human man (NOT a halfling -- adult proportions, just small) elbowing aggressively through a crowd, daggers at belt, eyes like a cornered predator. A gnome man with quick clever hands and darting calculating eyes browsing a market stall, his bag already slightly heavier than when he arrived.\n   123→\n   124→**Convergence** (Panel 32): Bird's-eye aerial view of the port town. Eight tiny silhouette figures approach from different directions through the streets, all converging on one location. The visual metaphor is explicit: all roads lead here.\n   125→\n   126→**The Recruitment Yard** (Panels 33-40): The ensemble assembles. A military training yard with scarlet banners, practice dummies, and one very small gnome named Finkle who makes up for his stature with absolute tyrannical authority. Finkle's entrance is a comedy set piece -- a door bangs open, and through it strides (minces) a tiny figure in an immaculate suit that still does not fit, trailed by two enormous half-ogre bodyguards. He commands from atop a wooden crate, presents a triangle diagram with FINKLE at the top and EVERYONE ELSE at the bottom, and introduces his bodyguards with the disdain of someone presenting unruly furniture. The recruits spar, size each other up, and are dismissed into the awkward groups that form when strangers are forced together.\n   127→\n   128→**The Tavern** (Panels 41-44): Evening. Warm golden light from tavern windows. Inside, the fragile social dynamics begin to form -- Tiny Rick (the 4-foot human) and Dallor (the market gnome) share grudging respect at the far end of a table. Then Jorgen arrives: a ranger in a torn gold military tunic SLAMS through the door, gasping, bleeding, one hand on the doorframe -- and behind him, the night is full of moving shapes. The tavern erupts into combat against swarming kobolds. This is the pilot's first action sequence, and it is chaotic, messy, and real.\n   129→\n   130→**Panel Breakdown**:\n   131→\n   132→| Panel | Title | Description | Characters | Mood | Camera |\n   133→|-------|-------|-------------|------------|------|--------|\n   134→| 21 | Sprocket -- The Study | Cramped moving carriage interior. Books everywhere, astronomical instruments, arcane diagrams on walls. A gnome diviner hunches over star charts, ink-stained hands, forgotten sandwich. | Sprocket | Obsessive focus, warmth | Mid interior, intimate |\n   135→| 22 | Sprocket -- The Arrival | Port town entrance. Sprocket among the crowd, small but grinning with analytical edge. Ornate robes, brass goggles on forehead. The town stretches before him. | Sprocket | Eager anticipation | Wide, street level |\n   136→| 23 | Brigit -- The Forest Edge | Dark scarred forest edge. Burned trunks with new green growth. A grey-brown wolf stands alert at the treeline, ears forward, weight on front paws. | Brigit (wolf form) | Wild tension | Mid-wide, forest edge |\n   137→| 24 | Brigit -- The Shift | Young woman completing wolf-to-human transformation at a stone town wall. Liquid, organic shift: traces of fur dissolving into pale freckled skin, green eyes mid-transition. | Brigit | Liminal, uncanny | Mid-shot, dramatic |\n   138→| 25 | Brigit -- The Town | Young woman walks barefoot through medieval streets. Dark auburn hair with leaves, pale skin, green scanning eyes. Jorgen -- human ranger in torn gold tunic -- stumbles through, gasping, face scratched, bleeding. Behind him through the door: the night is full of moving shapes. | Jorgen | Shock, violence arriving | Mid-shot, dramatic entrance |\n   159→| 44 | The Fight | Tavern erupts into combat. Kobolds swarm through windows and door. Recruits who were drinking grab weapons. Tables overturn. Chaos and broken glass. | Full ensemble, kobolds | Violent chaos | Wide action, dynamic angles |\n   160→\n   161→---\n   162→\n   163→### Act 5: The Descent (Panels 45-61)\n   164→\n   165→**Setting**: Tavern aftermath, then underground -- sewers, ancient corridors, trapped passages, spiral staircase.\n   166→**Tone**: Post-combat tension building to claustrophobic dread, punctuated by one moment of dragonborn authority.\n   167→**Color Temperature**: Warm amber (tavern) rapidly cooling to torchlit orange against black stone, ending in unnatural crimson.\n   168→\n   169→The tavern is wrecked. Dead kobolds, spilled ale, a smoldering tapestry. In the aftermath, a folded note is discovered -- fussy precise handwriting, lit by dim amber light. The party huddles around the table with the note at center, a single candle lighting their faces from below. The decision is made.\n   170→\n   171→Jorgen briefs them at the bar, then leads them to a hidden sewer entrance behind a nondescript building. The descent begins. The architecture changes as they go deeper: crude sewer walls give way to something older, precisely carved geometric patterns that predate the town by centuries. The air changes. The sounds change.\n   172→\n   173→They encounter kobolds in the dark -- hundreds of reptilian eyes reflecting their single torch. A standoff crystallizes: neither side moves. Then Stalwart steps forward and speaks in Draconic, his voice carrying the authority of his lineage. The kobolds retreat, parting like a tide.\n   174→\n   175→Deeper still. Trap corridors with visible pressure plates and suspicious grooves. Jorgen holds open a heavy stone door while the party rushes through. Then the ceiling begins to descend -- a massive stone slab dropping slowly, inexorably. The most claustrophobic panel in the pilot: a vertically compressed frame showing the ceiling terrifyingly close, and carved into its surface in dried blood, one word: KNEEL.\n   176→\n   177→A spiral staircase descends into darkness. The party is strung out along the spiral, each a level apart, connected by torchlight but separated by stone. At the bottom, red light hits them -- not torchlight, not fire, something supernatural. Their faces go crimson. Their expressions shift from exhaustion to recognition that they have arrived somewhere that should not exist.\n   178→\n   179→**Panel Breakdown**:\n   180→\n   181→| Panel | Title | Description | Characters | Mood | Camera |\n   182→|-------|-------|-------------|------------|------|--------|\n   183→| 45 | Tavern -- Aftermath | Wrecked tavern. Overturned tables, spilled ale, dead kobolds, smoldering tapestry. Warriors catch breath. Weapons still drawn.
